وَمِنْ كِتَابِ الْمَنَاسِكِ

Start reading ↓
Report450/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 107
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Ibrahim b. Uqbah, from Kurayb, freedman of Ibn Abbas, from Ibn Abbas ﵄, that

Matn

the Prophet ﷺ was returning, and when he was at al-Rawha' he met a company of riders and greeted them, and said: “Who are the people?” They said: “The Muslims; and who are the people?” He said: “The Messenger of God.” Then a woman lifted a boy of hers toward him from a litter (mihaffah) and said: “O Messenger of God, does this one have Hajj?” He said: “Yes, and you have a reward.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ كُرَيْبٍ مَوْلَى ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما،

Matn

أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَفَلَ، فَلَمَّا كَانَ بِالرَّوْحَاءِ لَقِيَ رَكْبًا فَسَلَّمَ عَلَيْهِمْ وَقَالَ: «مَنِ الْقَوْمُ؟» فَقَالُوا: الْمُسْلِمُونَ، فَمَنِ الْقَوْمُ؟ قَالَ: «رَسُولُ اللَّهِ». فَرَفَعَتْ إِلَيْهِ امْرَأَةٌ صَبِيًّا لَهَا مِنْ مِحَفَّةٍ فَقَالَتْ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، أَلِهَذَا حَجٌّ؟ فَقَالَ: «نَعَمْ، وَلَكِ أَجْرٌ»

  2. 02Kurayb, freedman of Ibn Abbasكُرَيْبٍ مَوْلَى ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ
  3. 03Ibrahim b. Uqbahإِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عُقْبَةَ
  4. 04Ibn Uyaynahابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ

Report451/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 107
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Ibrahim b. Uqbah, from Kurayb, freedman of Ibn Abbas, from Ibn Abbas ﵄, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ passed by a woman who was in her litter, and it was said to her: “This is the Messenger of God ﷺ.” So she took hold of the arms of a boy who was with her and said: “Does this one have Hajj?” He said: “Yes, and you have a reward.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ كُرَيْبٍ مَوْلَى ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَرَّ بِامْرَأَةٍ وَهِيَ فِي مِحَفَّتِهَا فَقِيلَ لَهَا: هَذَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم، فَأَخَذَتْ بِعَضُدَيْ صَبِيٍّ كَانَ مَعَهَا فَقَالَتْ: أَلِهَذَا حَجٌّ؟ قَالَ: «نَعَمْ، وَلَكِ أَجْرٌ»

Transmission overview

Transmission path

  2. 02Kurayb, freedman of Ibn Abbasكُرَيْبٍ مَوْلَى ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ
  3. 03Ibrahim b. Uqbahإِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عُقْبَةَ

Report452/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 107
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Malik b. Mighwal, from Abu al-Safar, who said:

Matn

Ibn Abbas said: “O people, let me hear what you say, and understand what I say to you: any slave whose owners performed Hajj with him and who died before being freed has fulfilled his Hajj, and if he was freed before he died, let him perform Hajj [again]; and any boy whose family performed Hajj with him and who died before reaching maturity has fulfilled his Hajj, and if he reaches maturity, let him perform Hajj.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ مِغْوَلٍ، عَنْ أَبِي السَّفَرِ قَالَ:

Matn

قَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ: «أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ، أَسْمِعُونِي مَا تَقُولُونَ، وَافْهَمُوا مَا أَقُولُ لَكُمْ، أَيُّمَا مَمْلُوكٍ حَجَّ بِهِ أَهْلُهُ فَمَاتَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُعْتَقَ فَقَدْ قَضَى حَجَّهُ، وَإِنْ عُتِقَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَمُوتَ فَلْيَحْجُجْ، وَأَيُّمَا غُلَامٍ حَجَّ بِهِ أَهْلُهُ فَمَاتَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُدْرِكَ فَقَدْ قَضَى حَجَّتَهُ، وَإِنْ بَلَغَ فَلْيَحْجُجْ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abu al-SafarEarliest transmitterأَبِي السَّفَرِ
  2. 02Malik b. Mighwalمَالِكِ بْنِ مِغْوَلٍ
  3. 03Sa'id ibn Salimسَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ

Report453/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 108
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, who said: I heard al-Zuhri narrating from Sulayman b. Yasar, from Ibn Abbas, that

Matn

a woman from Khath'am asked the Prophet ﷺ and said: “God's obligation of Hajj upon His servants has reached my father when he is a very old man, unable to remain steady on his mount; do you think I should perform Hajj on his behalf?” The Prophet ﷺ said: “Yes.” Sufyan said: This is how I memorized it from al-Zuhri. Amr b. Dinar informed me, from al-Zuhri, from Sulayman b. Yasar, from the Prophet ﷺ, the like of it, and he added in it: She said: “O Messenger of God, will that benefit him?” He said: “Yes, just as if he owed a debt and you paid it, it would benefit him.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ الزُّهْرِيَّ، يُحَدِّثُ عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ امْرَأَةً مِنْ خَثْعَمَ سَأَلَتِ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَتْ: إِنَّ فَرِيضَةَ اللَّهِ فِي الْحَجِّ عَلَى عِبَادِهِ أَدْرَكَتْ أَبِي شَيْخًا كَبِيرًا لَا يَسْتَطِيعُ أَنْ يَسْتَمْسِكَ عَلَى رَاحِلَتِهِ، فَهَلْ تَرَى أَنْ أَحُجَّ عَنْهُ؟ فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «نَعَمْ» قَالَ سُفْيَانُ: هَكَذَا حَفِظْتُهُ مِنَ الزُّهْرِيِّ أَخْبَرَنِي عَمْرُو بْنُ دِينَارٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَهُ وَزَادَ فِيهِ: فَقَالَتْ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، فَهَلْ يَنْفَعُهُ ذَلِكَ؟ قَالَ: «نَعَمْ، كَمَا لَوْ كَانَ عَلَيْهِ دَيْنٌ فَقَضَيْتِهِ نَفَعَهُ»

Transmission path

  2. 02Sulayman b. Yasarسُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ
  3. 03al-Zuhriالزُّهْرِيَّ
  4. 04Ibn Uyaynahابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ

1363215

Report454/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 108
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Sulayman b. Yasar, from Abdullah b. Abbas, who said:

Matn

Al-Fadl b. Abbas was riding behind the Messenger of God ﷺ, and a woman from Khath'am came to him seeking a fatwa. Al-Fadl began looking at her and she at him, and the Messenger of God ﷺ began turning al-Fadl's face to the other side. She said: “O Messenger of God, God's obligation of Hajj upon His servants has reached my father, a very old man who cannot stay steady on the mount; shall I perform Hajj on his behalf?” He said: “Yes.” And that was during the Farewell Pilgrimage.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ الْفَضْلُ بْنُ عَبَّاسٍ رَدِيفَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَجَاءَتْهُ امْرَأَةٌ مِنْ خَثْعَمَ تَسْتَفْتِيهِ، فَجَعَلَ الْفَضْلُ يَنْظُرُ إِلَيْهَا وَتَنْظُرُ إِلَيْهِ، فَجَعَلَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَصْرِفُ وَجْهَ الْفَضْلِ إِلَى الشِّقِّ الْآخَرِ، فَقَالَتْ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، فَرِيضَةُ اللَّهِ فِي الْحَجِّ عَلَى عِبَادِهِ أَدْرَكَتْ أَبِي شَيْخًا كَبِيرًا لَا يَسْتَطِيعُ أَنْ يَثْبُتَ عَلَى الرَّاحِلَةِ، أَفَأَحُجُّ عَنْهُ؟ قَالَ: «نَعَمْ». وَذَلِكَ فِي حَجَّةِ الْوَدَاعِ

  2. 02Sulayman b. Yasarسُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ

Report455/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 108
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid al-Zanji informed us, from Ibn Jurayj; Ibn Shihab said: Sulayman b. Yasar narrated to me, from Ibn Abbas, from al-Fadl b. Abbas, that

Matn

a woman from Khath'am said to the Messenger of God ﷺ: “My father has been reached by God's obligation of Hajj, and he is a very old man who cannot sit upright on the back of his camel.” He said: “Then perform Hajj on his behalf.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ الزَّنْجِيُّ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ: حَدَّثَنِي سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ يَسَارٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، عَنِ الْفَضْلِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ امْرَأَةً، مِنْ خَثْعَمَ قَالَتْ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: إِنَّ أَبِي قَدْ أَدْرَكَتْهُ فَرِيضَةُ اللَّهِ فِي الْحَجِّ وَهُوَ شَيْخٌ كَبِيرٌ لَا يَسْتَطِيعُ أَنْ يَسْتَوِيَ عَلَى ظَهْرِ بَعِيرِهِ، قَالَ: «فَحُجِّي عَنْهُ»

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01al-Fadl b. AbbasEarliest transmitterالْفَضْلِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ
  2. 02Ibn Abbasابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ
  3. 03Ibn Shihabسُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ يَسَارٍ
  4. 04Ibn Jurayjابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ
  5. 05Muslim b. Khalid al-Zanjiمُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ الزَّنْجِيُّ

1363217

Report456/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 108
English
Isnad

Amr b. Abi Salamah informed us, from Abd al-Aziz b. Muhammad, from Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith al-Makhzumi, from Zayd b. Ali b. Husayn, from his father, from Ubaydullah b. Abi Rafi', from Ali b. Abi Talib ﵁, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“And all of Mina is a place of slaughter.” Then ⦗p. 109⦘ a woman from Khath'am came to him and said: “My father is an old man who has become senile, and God's obligation upon His servants regarding Hajj has reached him, and he is unable to perform it; will it suffice for him if I perform it on his behalf?” He said: “Yes.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا عَمْرُو بْنُ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ الْمَخْزُومِيِّ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ عَلِيِّ بْنِ حُسَيْنٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي رَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَلِيِّ بْنِ أَبِي طَالِبٍ رضي الله عنه، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«وَكُلُّ مِنًى مَنْحَرٌ». ثُمَّ ⦗ص: 109⦘ جَاءَتْهُ امْرَأَةٌ مِنْ خَثْعَمَ فَقَالَتْ: إِنَّ أَبِي شَيْخٌ قَدْ أَفْنَدَ وَأَدْرَكَتْهُ فَرِيضَةُ اللَّهِ عَلَى عِبَادِهِ فِي الْحَجِّ وَلَا يَسْتَطِيعُ أَدَاءَهَا، فَهَلْ يَجْزِي عَنْهُ أَنْ أُؤَدِّيَهَا عَنْهُ؟ قَالَ: «نَعَمْ»

  2. 02Ubaydullah b. Abi Rafi'عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي رَافِعٍ
  3. 03his fatherأَبِيهِ
  4. 04Zayd b. Ali b. Husaynزَيْدِ بْنِ عَلِيِّ بْنِ حُسَيْنٍ
  5. 05Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith al-Makhzumiعَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ الْمَخْزُومِيِّ

Report457/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 109
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Handhalah, who said: I heard Tawus say:

Matn

A woman came to the Prophet ﷺ and said: “My mother died while Hajj was due from her,” and he said: “Perform Hajj on behalf of your mother.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ حَنْظَلَةَ قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ طَاوُسًا، يَقُولُ:

Matn

أَتَتِ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم امْرَأَةٌ فَقَالَتْ: إِنَّ أُمِّي مَاتَتْ وَعَلَيْهَا حَجٌّ، فَقَالَ: «حُجِّي عَنْ أُمِّكِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01TawusEarliest transmitterطَاوُسًا
  2. 02Handhalahحَنْظَلَةَ
  3. 03Sa'id ibn Salimسَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ

Report458/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 109
English
Isnad

Muslim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata':

Matn

The Prophet ﷺ heard a man saying: “Labbayk [Here I am at Your service] on behalf of so-and-so,” and the Prophet ﷺ said: “If you have performed Hajj, then say the talbiyah for him; otherwise, perform Hajj [first].”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ:

Matn

سَمِعَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَجُلًا يَقُولُ: لَبَّيْكَ عَنْ فُلَانٍ، فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «إِنْ كُنْتَ حَجَجْتَ فَلَبِّ عَنْهُ، وَإِلَّا فَاحْجُجْ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Ata'Earliest transmitterعَطَاءٍ
  2. 02Ibn Jurayjابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ
  3. 03Muslimمُسْلِمٌ

Report459/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 109
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibrahim b. Yazid, from Muhammad b. Abbad b. Ja'far, who said:

Matn

We sat with Abdullah b. Umar, and I heard him say: A man asked the Messenger of God ﷺ: “Who is the pilgrim?” He said: “The disheveled, the unperfumed (al-sha'ith al-tafil).” Another stood and said: “O Messenger of God, which Hajj is best?” He said: “The raising of voices in talbiyah and the shedding of blood [of sacrifices] (al-'ajj wa al-thajj).” Another stood and said: “O Messenger of God, what is the ‘way’ (al-sabil)?” He said: “Provision and a mount.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ يَزِيدَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبَّادِ بْنِ جَعْفَرٍ قَالَ:

Matn

قَعَدْنَا إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فَسَمِعْتُهُ يَقُولُ: سَأَلَ رَجُلٌ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ: مَا الْحَاجُّ؟ قَالَ: «الشَّعِثُ التَّفِلُ». فَقَامَ آخَرُ فَقَالَ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، أَيُّ الْحَجِّ أَفْضَلُ؟ قَالَ: «الْعَجُّ وَالثَّجُّ». فَقَامَ آخَرُ فَقَالَ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، مَا السَّبِيلُ؟ قَالَ: «زَادٌ وَرَاحِلَةٌ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Muhammad b. Abbad b. Ja'farEarliest transmitterمُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبَّادِ بْنِ جَعْفَرٍ
  2. 02Ibrahim b. Yazidإِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ يَزِيدَ
  3. 03Sa'id ibn Salimسَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ

Report460/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 109
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Sufyan al-Thawri, from Tariq b. Abd al-Rahman, from Abdullah b. Abi Awfa, companion of the Prophet ﷺ, that he said:

Matn

I asked him about a man who has not performed Hajj: may he borrow money for Hajj? He said: “No.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ سُفْيَانَ الثَّوْرِيِّ، عَنْ طَارِقِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي أَوْفَى، صَاحِبِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

سَأَلْتُهُ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ لَمْ يَحُجَّ، أَيَسْتَقْرِضُ لِلْحَجِّ؟ قَالَ: «لَا»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01Abdullah b. Abi Awfa, companion of the ProphetEarliest transmitterعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي أَوْفَى، صَاحِبِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله
  2. 02Tariq b. Abd al-Rahmanطَارِقِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ
  3. 03Sufyan al-Thawriسُفْيَانَ الثَّوْرِيِّ
  4. 04Sa'id ibn Salimسَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ

Report461/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 109
English
Isnad

Muslim and Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata', from Ibn Abbas, that

Matn

a man asked him: “I hire myself out to these people and perform the rites with them; do I have a reward?” Ibn Abbas said: “Yes: ‘Those will have a share of what they have earned, and God is swift in reckoning’ [Al-Baqarah: 202].”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، وَسَعِيدٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، سَأَلَهُ فَقَالَ: " أُؤَاجِرُ نَفْسِي مِنْ هَؤُلَاءِ الْقَوْمِ فَأَنْسُكُ مَعَهُمُ الْمَنَاسِكَ أَلِي أَجْرٌ؟ فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ: نَعَمْ، {أُولَئِكَ لَهُمْ نَصِيبٌ مِمَّا كَسَبُوا وَاللَّهُ سَرِيعُ الْحِسَابِ} [الْبَقَرَة: 202] "

  2. 02Ata'عَطَاءٍ
  3. 03Ibn Jurayjابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ
  4. 04Muslim and Sa'idمُسْلِمٌ، وَسَعِيدٌ

Report462/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 109
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata', who said:

Matn

The Prophet ﷺ heard a man saying: “Labbayk on behalf of so-and-so,” and the Prophet ﷺ said to him: “If you have performed Hajj, say the talbiyah for him; otherwise perform Hajj for yourself, then perform Hajj for him.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ قَالَ:

Matn

سَمِعَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَجُلًا يَقُولُ: لَبَّيْكَ عَنْ فُلَانٍ، فَقَالَ لَهُ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «إِنْ كُنْتَ حَجَجْتَ فَلَبِّ عَنْهُ، وَإِلَّا فَاحْجُجْ عَنْ نَفْسِكِ ثُمَّ احْجُجْ عَنْهُ»

Report463/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 110
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ayyub, from Abu Qilabah, who said:

Matn

“Ibn Abbas heard a man saying: ‘Labbayk on behalf of Shubrumah,’ and Ibn Abbas said: ‘Woe to you! Who is Shubrumah?’ He mentioned a relative of his. He said to him: ‘Have you performed Hajj for yourself?’ He said: ‘No.’ He said: ‘Then perform Hajj for yourself, then perform Hajj for Shubrumah.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلَابَةَ قَالَ:

Matn

" سَمِعَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ، رَجُلًا يَقُولُ: لَبَّيْكَ عَنْ شُبْرُمَةَ، فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ: وَيْحَكَ وَمَا شُبْرُمَةُ؟ قَالَ: فَذَكَرَ قَرَابَةً لَهُ، فَقَالَ لَهُ: أَحَجَجْتَ عَنْ نَفْسِكَ؟ قَالَ: لَا، قَالَ: فَاحْجُجْ عَنْ نَفْسِكِ ثُمَّ احْجُجْ عَنْ شُبْرُمَةَ "

  2. 02Ayyubأَيُّوبَ

Report464/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 110
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata' and Tawus, that both said:

Matn

“The obligatory Hajj is [paid] from the principal of the estate.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ، وَطَاوُسٍ، أَنَّهُمَا قَالَا:

Matn

«الْحِجَّةُ الْوَاجِبَةُ مِنْ رَأْسِ الْمَالِ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest

  2. 02Ibn Jurayjابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ
  3. 03Muslim b. Khalidمُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ

Report465/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 110
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid and another informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said: Ata' informed me that he heard Jabir b. Abdullah say:

Matn

Ali ﵁ came back from his [zakah-collection] mission, and the Prophet ﷺ said to him: “With what did you enter ihram, O Ali?” He said: “With what the Prophet ﷺ entered ihram.” He said: “Then offer a sacrificial animal (hady), and remain in ihram as you are.” He said: So Ali offered a sacrificial animal for him.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، وَغَيْرُهُ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي عَطَاءٌ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ جَابِرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ قَالَ:

Matn

قَدِمَ عَلِيٌّ رضي الله عنه مِنْ سِعَايَتِهِ فَقَالَ لَهُ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «بِمَ أَهْلَلْتَ يَا عَلِيُّ؟» قَالَ: بِمَا أَهَلَّ بِهِ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم، قَالَ: «فَأَهْدِ، وَامْكُثْ حَرَامًا كَمَا أَنْتَ». قَالَ: فَأَهْدَى لَهُ عَلِيٌّ هَدْيًا

Transmission overview

Transmission path

  2. 02Ata'عَطَاءٌ

Dār al-Kutub al-ʿIlmiyya, Beirut, 1400 AH (1980), corrected against the Būlāq and Indian printings
Edition page
Vol. 1, p. 110
Source record
1363227
Permalink ↗
Report466/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 110
English
Isnad

Muslim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ja'far b. Muhammad, from his father, from Jabir, as he was narrating about the Hajj of the Prophet ﷺ, who said:

Matn

We set out with the Prophet ﷺ until, when we were at al-Bayda', I looked as far as my sight reached, at riders and walkers, in front of him, to his right, to his left and behind him, all of them wanting to follow him, seeking to say as the Messenger of God ﷺ said, intending nothing but Hajj, and 'Umrah was not known [to us]. When we had circumambulated and were at al-Marwah, he said: “O people, whoever has no sacrificial animal with him, let him come out of ihram and make it an 'Umrah; and had I known beforehand of my affair what I came to know afterward, I would not have brought sacrificial animals.” So those who had no sacrificial animal with them came out of ihram.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، وَهُوَ يُحَدِّثُ عَنْ حَجَّةِ النَّبِيِّ، صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

خَرَجْنَا مَعَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم حَتَّى إِذَا كُنَّا بِالْبَيْدَاءِ فَنَظَرْتُ مَدَّ بَصَرِي مِنْ بَيْنِ رَاكِبٍ وَرَاجِلٍ، بَيْنَ يَدَيْهِ، وَعَنْ يَمِينِهِ، وَعَنْ شِمَالِهِ، وَمِنْ وَرَائِهِ، كُلُّهُمْ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَأْتَمَّ بِهِ، يَلْتَمِسُ أَنْ يَقُولَ كَمَا يَقُولُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم، لَا يَنْوِي إِلَّا الْحَجَّ وَلَا يُعْرَفُ الْعُمْرَةَ، فَلَمَّا طُفْنَا فَكُنَّا عِنْدَ الْمَرْوَةِ قَالَ: «أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ، مَنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ مَعَهُ هَدْيٌ فَلْيَحْلِلْ وَلْيَجْعَلْهَا عُمْرَةً، وَلَوِ اسْتَقْبَلْتُ مِنْ أَمْرِي مَا اسْتَدْبَرْتُ مَا أَهْدَيْتُ». فَحَلَّ مَنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ مَعَهُ هَدْيٌ

Report467/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 110
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Mansur b. Abd al-Rahman, from Safiyyah bint Shaybah, from Asma bint Abi Bakr ﵃, who said:

Matn

We set out with the Messenger of God ﷺ, and the Prophet ﷺ said: “Whoever has a sacrificial animal with him, let him remain in his ihram, and whoever has no sacrificial animal, let him come out of ihram.” I had no sacrificial animal, so I came out of ihram, while al-Zubayr had a sacrificial animal and did not ⦗p. 111⦘ come out of ihram.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ مَنْصُورِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ صَفِيَّةَ بِنْتِ شَيْبَةَ، عَنْ أَسْمَاءَ بِنْتِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ، رَضِيَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُمْ قَالَتْ:

Matn

خَرَجْنَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «مَنْ كَانَ مَعَهُ هَدْيٌ فَلْيَقُمْ عَلَى إِحْرَامِهِ، وَمَنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ مَعَهُ هَدْيٌ فَلْيَحْلِلْ». وَلَمْ يَكُنْ مَعِي هَدْيٌ فَحَلَلْتُ، وَكَانَ مَعَ الزُّبَيْرِ هَدْيٌ فَلَمْ ⦗ص: 111⦘ يَحْلِلْ

Report468/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 111
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Yahya b. Sa'id, from Amrah, from A'ishah ﵂, who said:

Matn

We set out with the Prophet ﷺ five [nights] before the end of Dhu al-Qa'dah, thinking of nothing but Hajj. When we were at Sarif, or near it, the Prophet ﷺ commanded those who had no sacrificial animal with them to make it an 'Umrah. When we were at Mina, I was brought beef, and I said: “What is this?” They said: “The Messenger of God ﷺ sacrificed [it] on behalf of his wives.” Yahya said: I narrated it to al-Qasim b. Muhammad, and he said: “By God, she has brought you the hadith in its proper form.” Malik informed us, from Yahya, from Amrah and al-Qasim, the like of the hadith of Sufyan, not differing in its meaning.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، رَضِيَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهَا قَالَتْ:

Matn

خَرَجْنَا مَعَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِخَمْسٍ بَقِينَ مِنْ ذِي الْقَعْدَةِ لَا نَرَى إِلَّا الْحَجَّ، فَلَمَّا كُنَّا بِسَرِفَ أَوْ قَرِيبًا مِنْهَا أَمَرَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ مَعَهُ هَدْيٌ أَنْ يَجْعَلَهَا عُمْرَةً، فَلَمَّا كُنَّا بِمِنًى أُتِيتُ بِلَحْمِ بَقَرٍ فَقُلْتُ: مَا هَذَا؟ قَالُوا: ذَبَحَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ نِسَائِهِ قَالَ يَحْيَى: فَحَدَّثْتُ بِهِ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ فَقَالَ: جَاءَتْكَ وَاللَّهِ بِالْحَدِيثِ عَلَى وَجْهِهِ أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ، وَالْقَاسِمِ، بِمِثْلِ حَدِيثِ سُفْيَانَ لَا يُخَالِفُ مَعْنَاهُ

Report469/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 111
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Abd al-Rahman b. al-Qasim, from his father, from A'ishah ﵂, that she said:

Matn

We set out with the Messenger of God ﷺ on a Hajj, thinking of nothing but Hajj, until, when we were at Sarif or near it, I began menstruating. The Messenger of God ﷺ came in to me while I was weeping and said: “What is the matter with you? Have you begun your period?” I said: “Yes.” He said: “This is a matter God has decreed for the daughters of Adam; so do what the pilgrim does, except that you do not circumambulate the House.” She said: And the Messenger of God ﷺ sacrificed cows on behalf of his wives.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ:

Matn

خَرَجْنَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي حَجَّةٍ لَا نَرَى إِلَّا الْحَجَّ، حَتَّى إِذَا كُنَّا بِسَرِفَ أَوْ قَرِيبًا مِنْهَا حِضْتُ، فَدَخَلَ عَلَيَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَأَنَا أَبْكِي فَقَالَ: «مَالَكِ؟ أَنَفِسْتِ؟» قُلْتُ: نَعَمْ، فَقَالَ: «إِنَّ هَذَا أَمْرٌ كَتَبَهُ اللَّهُ عَلَى بَنَاتِ آدَمَ، فَاقْضِي مَا يَقْضِي الْحَاجُّ غَيْرَ أَنْ لَا تَطُوفِي بِالْبَيْتِ». قَالَتْ: وَضَحَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ نِسَائِهِ الْبَقَرَ

Report470/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 111
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us: Ibn Tawus, Ibrahim b. Maysarah and Hisham b. Hujayr narrated to us; they heard Tawus say:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ left Medina without naming either Hajj or 'Umrah, awaiting the decree [of God]; and when he was between al-Safa and al-Marwah, he commanded those of his companions who had entered ihram and had no sacrificial animal with them to make it an 'Umrah, and he said: “Had I known beforehand of my affair what I came to know afterward, I would not have driven the sacrificial animal; but I have matted my hair and driven my sacrificial animal, so there is no release for me before the release of my sacrificial animal [i.e., its slaughter].” Suraqah b. Malik stood up to him and said: “O Messenger of God, decide for us as [for] a people who were born today: is this 'Umrah of ours ⦗p. 112⦘ for this year of ours or for all time?” He said: “Rather for all time: the 'Umrah has entered into the Hajj until the Day of Resurrection.” He said: And Ali ﵁ came from Yemen, and the Prophet ﷺ said to him: “With what did you enter ihram?” One of the two [narrators] said, from Tawus: “Labbayk, with an intention like the intention of the Prophet ﷺ.” And the other said: “Labbayk, for a Hajj like the Hajj of the Prophet ﷺ.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ طَاوُسٍ، وَإِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ مَيْسَرَةَ، وَهِشَامُ بْنُ حُجَيْرٍ، سَمِعُوا طَاوُسًا، يَقُولُ:

Matn

خَرَجَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِنَ الْمَدِينَةِ لَا يُسَمِّي حَجًّا وَلَا عُمْرَةً يَنْتَظِرُ الْقَضَاءَ وَهُوَ بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ، فَأَمَرَ أَصْحَابَهُ مَنْ كَانَ مِنْهُمْ أَهَلَّ وَلَمْ يَكُنْ مَعَهُ هَدْيٌ أَنْ يَجْعَلَهَا عُمْرَةً، وَقَالَ: «لَوِ اسْتَقْبَلْتُ مِنْ أَمْرِي مَا اسْتَدْبَرْتُ لَمَا سُقْتَ الْهَدْيَ، وَلَكِنْ لَبَّدْتُ رَأْسِي وَسُقْتُ هَدْيِي فَلَيْسَ لِي مَحِلٌّ دُونَ مَحِلِّ هَدْيِي». فَقَامَ إِلَيْهِ سُرَاقَةُ بْنُ مَالِكٍ فَقَالَ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، اقْضِ لَنَا قَضَاءَ قَوْمٍ كَأَنَّمَا وُلِدُوا الْيَوْمَ، أَعُمْرَتُنَا ⦗ص: 112⦘ هَذِهِ لِعَامِنَا هَذَا أَمْ لِلْأَبَدِ؟ قَالَ: «بَلْ لِلْأَبَدِ، دَخَلَتِ الْعُمْرَةُ فِي الْحَجِّ إِلَى يَوْمِ الْقِيَامَةِ». قَالَ: وَدَخَلَ عَلِيٌّ رضي الله عنه مِنَ الْيَمَنِ فَقَالَ لَهُ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «بِمَ أَهْلَلْتَ؟» فَقَالَ أَحَدُهُمَا عَنْ طَاوُسٍ: لَبَّيْكَ إِهْلَالًا كَإِهْلَالِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم. وَقَالَ الْآخَرُ: لَبَّيْكَ حَجَّةً كَحَجَّةِ النَّبِيِّ صَلَّى اللهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ

English
Isnad

Muslim and Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata', that

Matn

a man asked Ibn Abbas: “I hire myself out to these people and perform the rites with them; will it suffice for me?” Ibn Abbas said: “Yes: ‘Those will have a share of what they have earned, and God is swift in reckoning’ [Al-Baqarah: 202].”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، وَسَعِيدٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، سَأَلَ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ فَقَالَ: " أُؤَاجِرُ نَفْسِي مِنْ هَؤُلَاءِ الْقَوْمِ، فَأَنْسُكُ مَعَهُمُ الْمَنَاسِكَ، هَلْ يُجْزِي عَنِّي؟ فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ: نَعَمْ، {أُولَئِكَ لَهُمْ نَصِيبٌ مِمَّا كَسَبُوا وَاللَّهُ سَرِيعُ الْحِسَابِ} [الْبَقَرَة: 202] "

English
Isnad

Al-Qaddah informed us, from Sufyan al-Thawri, from Zayd b. Jubayr, who said:

Matn

“I was with Abdullah b. Umar when he was asked about this [matter], and he said: ‘This is the Hajj of Islam; so let him seek to fulfill his vow.’” That is, [regarding] one upon whom Hajj was due and who vowed a Hajj.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا الْقَدَّاحُ، عَنْ سُفْيَانَ الثَّوْرِيِّ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ قَالَ:

Matn

" إِنِّي لَعِنْدَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ وَسُئِلَ عَنْ هَذِهِ، فَقَالَ: هَذِهِ حَجَّةُ الْإِسْلَامِ، فَلْيَلْتَمِسْ أَنْ يَقْضِيَ نَذْرَهُ. يَعْنِي: لِمَنْ كَانَ عَلَيْهِ الْحَجُّ وَنَذَرَ حَجًّا "

He said: Sa'id ibn Salim said: And he argued [for this] that Sufyan al-Thawri informed him, from Mu'awiyah b. Ishaq, from Abu Salih al-Hanafi, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Isnad

Matn

«الْحَجُّ جِهَادٌ، وَالْعُمْرَةُ تَطَوَّعٌ»

Report475/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 112
English
Isnad

The translation is shown whole: its chain opens the passage below.

Matn

Ibn Uyaynah informed us that he heard Amr b. Dinar say: I heard Amr b. Aws say: Abd al-Rahman b. Abi Bakr ﵄ informed me that the Prophet ﷺ commanded him to take A'ishah ﵂ behind him [on his mount] and have her perform 'Umrah from al-Tan'im.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ عَمْرَو بْنَ دِينَارٍ، يَقُولُ: سَمِعْتُ عَمْرَو بْنَ أَوْسٍ، يَقُولُ: أَخْبَرَنِي عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ أَبِي بَكْرٍ رضي الله عنهما،

Matn

أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَمَرَهُ أَنْ يُرْدِفَ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها فَيُعْمِرَهَا مِنَ التَّنْعِيمِ

Report476/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 112
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Isma'il b. Umayyah, from Muzahim, from Abd al-Aziz b. Abdullah b. Khalid, from Muharrish al-Ka'bi, that

Matn

the Prophet ﷺ left al-Ji'ranah at night and performed 'Umrah, and in the morning was there like one who had spent the night ⦗p. 113⦘ Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, this hadith with this chain. Ibn Jurayj said: It is Muharrish. Al-Shafi'i ﵁ said: Ibn Jurayj was right, because his descendants among us are the Banu Muharrish.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ، عَنْ مُزَاحِمٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ، عَنْ مُحَرِّشٍ الْكَعْبِيِّ،

Matn

أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم خَرَجَ مِنَ الْجِعْرَانَةِ لَيْلًا فَاعْتَمَرَ وَأَصْبَحَ بِهَا كَبَائِتٍ ⦗ص: 113⦘ أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، هَذَا الْحَدِيثَ بِهَذَا الْإِسْنَادِ. قَالَ ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ: هُوَ مُحَرِّشٌ، قَالَ الشَّافِعِيُّ رضي الله عنه: وَأَصَابَ ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ لِأَنَّ وَلَدَهُ عِنْدَنَا بَنُو مُحَرِّشٍ

Report477/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 113
English
Isnad

Muslim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata', that

Matn

the Prophet ﷺ said to A'ishah: “Your circumambulation of the House and between al-Safa and al-Marwah suffices you for your Hajj and your 'Umrah.” Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Ibn Abi Najih, from Ata', from A'ishah ﵂, from the Prophet ﷺ, the like of it. And sometimes Sufyan said: “from Ata', from A'ishah ﵂”; and sometimes he said: “that the Prophet ﷺ said to A'ishah ﵂.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ لِعَائِشَةَ: «طَوَافُكِ بِالْبَيْتِ وَبَيْنَ الصَّفَا، وَالْمَرْوَةِ يَكْفِيكِ لِحَجِّكِ وَعُمْرَتِكِ» أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي نَجِيحٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِثْلَهُ. وَرُبَّمَا قَالَ سُفْيَانُ: عَنْ عَطَاءٍ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها. وَرُبَّمَا قَالَ: أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ لِعَائِشَةَ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ عَنْهَا

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 113
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Ibn Abi Husayn, from one of the sons of Anas b. Malik, who said:

Matn

We were with Anas b. Malik in Mecca, and whenever the hair of his head had grown back [after shaving], he would go out and perform 'Umrah.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي حُسَيْنٍ، عَنْ بَعْضِ وَلَدِ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ قَالَ:

Matn

كُنَّا مَعَ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ بِمَكَّةَ فَكَانَ إِذَا حَمَّمَ رَأْسُهُ خَرَجَ فَاعْتَمَرَ

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 113
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Sadaqah b. Yasar, from al-Qasim b. Muhammad, that

Matn

A'ishah, wife of the Prophet ﷺ, performed 'Umrah twice in one year. Sadaqah said: I said: “Did anyone find fault with her for that?” He said: “Glory be to God! The Mother of the Believers!” And I felt ashamed.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ صَدَقَةَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم اعْتَمَرَتْ فِي سَنَةٍ مَرَّتَيْنِ. قَالَ صَدَقَةُ: فَقُلْتُ: هَلْ عَابَ ذَلِكَ عَلَيْهَا أَحَدٌ؟ قَالَ: سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ، أُمُّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ فَاسْتَحْيَيْتُ

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 114
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. Uyaynah informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Salim b. Abdullah, from his father, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“The people of Medina enter ihram from Dhu al-Hulayfah, the people of Syria from al-Juhfah, and the people of Najd from Qarn.” Ibn Umar said: “And they claim that the Messenger of God ﷺ said: ‘And the people of Yemen enter ihram from Yalamlam.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«يُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الْمَدِينَةِ مِنْ ذِي الْحُلَيْفَةِ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الشَّامِ مِنَ الْجُحْفَةِ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ نَجْدٍ مِنْ قَرْنٍ». قَالَ ابْنُ عُمَرَ: وَيَزْعُمُونَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: «وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الْيَمَنِ مِنْ يَلَمْلَمَ»

English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Abdullah b. Dinar, from Ibn Umar, that he said:

Matn

The people of Medina were commanded to enter ihram from Dhu al-Hulayfah, the people of Syria from al-Juhfah, and the people of Najd from Qarn. Ibn Umar said: “As for these three, I heard them from the Messenger of God ﷺ, and I was told that the Messenger of God ﷺ said: ‘And the people of Yemen enter ihram from Yalamlam.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

أُمِرَ أَهْلُ الْمَدِينَةِ أَنْ يُهِلُّوا مِنْ ذِي الْحُلَيْفَةِ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الشَّامِ مِنَ الْجُحْفَةِ، وَأَهْلُ نَجْدٍ مِنْ قَرْنٍ. قَالَ ابْنُ عُمَرَ: أَمَّا هَؤُلَاءِ الثَّلَاثُ فَسَمِعْتُهُنَّ مِنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم، وَأُخْبِرْتُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: «وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الْيَمَنِ مِنْ يَلَمْلَمَ»

Report485/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 114
English
Isnad

Muslim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Nafi', from Ibn Umar ﵄, who said:

Matn

A man from the people of Medina stood up in the mosque in Medina and said: “O Messenger of God, from where do you command us to enter ihram?” He said: “The people of Medina enter ihram from Dhu al-Hulayfah, the people of Syria from al-Juhfah, and the people of Najd from Qarn.” Nafi' said to me: “And they claim that the Prophet ﷺ said: ‘And the people of Yemen enter ihram from Yalamlam.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ رضي الله عنهما قَالَ:

Matn

قَامَ رَجُلٌ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْمَدِينَةِ بِالْمَدِينَةِ فِي الْمَسْجِدِ فَقَالَ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، مِنْ أَيْنَ تَأْمُرُنَا أَنَّ نُهِلَّ؟ قَالَ: «يُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الْمَدِينَةِ مِنْ ذِي الْحُلَيْفَةِ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الشَّامِ مِنَ الْجُحْفَةِ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ نَجْدٍ مِنْ قَرْنٍ». قَالَ لِي نَافِعٌ: وَيَزْعُمُونَ أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: «وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الْيَمَنِ مِنْ يَلَمْلَمَ»

Report486/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 114
English
Isnad

The translation is shown whole: its chain opens the passage below.

Matn

Muslim and Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said: Abu al-Zubayr informed me that he heard Jabir b. Abdullah being asked about the place of ihram, and he said: “I heard him” — then he stopped; I think he meant the Prophet ﷺ — “say: ‘The people of Medina enter ihram from Dhu al-Hulayfah, and the other road [is] from al-Juhfah, and [that of] the people of the West; the people of Iraq enter ihram from Dhat Irq, the people of Najd from Qarn, and the people of Yemen from Yalamlam.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، وَسَعِيدٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي أَبُو الزُّبَيْرِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ جَابِرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ،

Matn

يُسْأَلُ عَنِ الْمُهِلِّ، فَقَالَ: سَمِعْتُهُ، ثُمَّ انْتَهَى أُرَاهُ يُرِيدُ النَّبِيَّ، صلى الله عليه وسلم، يَقُولُ: «يُهِلُّ أَهْلِ الْمَدِينَةِ مِنْ ذِي الْحُلَيْفَةِ، وَالطَّرِيقُ الْأُخْرَى مِنَ الْجُحْفَةِ وَأَهْلُ الْمَغْرِبِ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الْعِرَاقِ مِنْ ذَاتِ عِرْقٍ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ نَجْدٍ مِنْ قَرْنٍ، وَيُهِلُّ أَهْلُ الْيَمَنِ مِنْ يَلَمْلَمَ»

Report487/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 114
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us: Ibn Jurayj informed me:

Matn

Ata' informed me that the Messenger of God ﷺ appointed Dhu al-Hulayfah as the miqat for the people of Medina, al-Juhfah for the people of the West, and for the people of ⦗p. 115⦘ the East Dhat Irq, and for the people of Najd Qarn, and for whoever travels via Najd from the people of Yemen and others, Qarn al-Manazil, and for the people of Yemen Yalamlam. Muslim and Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said: I questioned Ata' again and said: “They claim that the Prophet ﷺ did not appoint Dhat Irq, and there were no people of the East at that time.” He said: “So we heard, that he appointed Dhat Irq or al-Aqiq for the people of the East.” He said: “And there was no Iraq at that time, but [it was] for the people of the East.” And he did not attribute it to anyone other than the Prophet ﷺ, but he insisted that the Prophet ﷺ appointed it.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي عَطَاءٌ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَقَّتَ لِأَهْلِ الْمَدِينَةِ ذَا الْحُلَيْفَةِ، وَلِأَهْلِ الْمَغْرِبِ الْجُحْفَةَ، وَلِأَهْلِ ⦗ص: 115⦘ الْمَشْرِقِ ذَاتَ عِرْقٍ، وَلِأَهْلِ نَجْدٍ قَرْنًا، وَمَنْ سَلَكَ نَجْدًا مِنْ أَهْلِ الْيَمَنِ وَغَيْرِهِمْ قَرْنَ الْمَنَازِلِ، وَلِأَهْلِ الْيَمَنِ يَلَمْلَمَ " أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، وَسَعِيدٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ: فَرَاجَعْتُ عَطَاءً فَقُلْتُ: إِنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم زَعَمُوا لَمْ يُوَقِّتْ ذَاتَ عِرْقٍ، وَلَمْ يَكُنْ أَهْلُ الْمَشْرِقِ حِينَئِذٍ، قَالَ: كَذَلِكَ سَمِعْنَا، أَنَّهُ وَقَّتَ ذَاتَ عِرْقٍ أَوِ الْعَقِيقِ لِأَهْلِ الْمَشْرِقِ. قَالَ: وَلَمْ يَكُنْ عِرَاقٌ يَوْمَئِذٍ وَلَكِنْ لِأَهْلِ الْمَشْرِقِ، وَلَمْ يَعْزُهُ إِلَى أَحَدٍ دُونَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم، وَلَكِنَّهُ يَأْبَى إِلَّا أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَقَّتَهُ

Report488/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 115
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ibn Tawus, from his father, who said:

Matn

“The Messenger of God ﷺ did not appoint Dhat Irq, and there were no people of the East at that time, so the people appointed Dhat Irq.” Al-Shafi'i ﵁ said: “And I think it is only as Tawus said, and God knows best.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ طَاوُسٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ قَالَ:

Matn

«لَمْ يُوَقِّتْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ذَاتَ عِرْقٍ، وَلَمْ يَكُنْ حِينَئِذٍ أَهْلُ مُشْرِقٍ، فَوَقَّتَ النَّاسُ ذَاتَ عِرْقٍ» قَالَ الشَّافِعِيُّ رضي الله عنه: وَلَا أَحْسِبُهُ إِلَّا كَمَا قَالَ طَاوُسٌ، وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ

Report489/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 115
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Amr b. Dinar, from Abu al-Sha'tha', that he said:

Matn

“The Messenger of God ﷺ did not appoint anything for the people of the East, so the people took Dhat Irq, opposite Qarn.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الشَّعْثَاءِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

«لَمْ يُوَقِّتْ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِأَهْلِ الْمَشْرِقِ شَيْئًا، فَاتَّخَذَ النَّاسُ بِحِيَالِ قَرْنٍ ذَاتَ عِرْقٍ»

Report490/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 115
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Ibn Tawus, from his father, who said:

Matn

The Messenger of God ﷺ appointed Dhu al-Hulayfah for the people of Medina, al-Juhfah for the people of Syria, Qarn for the people of Najd, and Alamlam for the people of Yemen. Then the Messenger of God ﷺ said: “These miqats are for their people, and for everyone who comes by them from other than their people, of those who intend Hajj and 'Umrah; and whoever's home is within [i.e., on this side of] that miqat, let him enter ihram from where he sets out, until it comes to the people of Mecca [who enter ihram from Mecca itself].” A trustworthy person informed us, from Ma'mar, from Ibn Tawus, from his father, from Ibn Abbas ﵄ ⦗p. 116⦘, from the Prophet ﷺ, concerning the miqats, the like of the meaning of Sufyan's hadith on the miqats.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ طَاوُسٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ قَالَ:

Matn

وَقَّتَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِأَهْلِ الْمَدِينَةِ ذَا الْحُلَيْفَةِ، وَلِأَهْلِ الشَّامِ الْجُحْفَةَ، وَلِأَهْلِ نَجْدٍ قَرْنًا، وَلِأَهْلِ الْيَمَنِ أَلَمْلَمَ، ثُمَّ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «هَذِهِ الْمَوَاقِيتُ لِأَهْلِهَا وَلِكُلِّ آتٍ أَتَى عَلَيْهَا مِنْ غَيْرِ أَهْلِهَا مِمَّنْ أَرَادَ الْحَجَّ وَالْعُمْرَةَ، وَمَنْ كَانَ أَهْلُهُ مِنْ دُونِ ذَلِكَ الْمِيقَاتِ فَلْيُهِلَّ مِنْ حَيْثُ يُنْشِئُ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَ ذَلِكَ عَلَى أَهْلِ مَكَّةَ» أَخْبَرَنَا الثِّقَةُ، عَنْ مَعْمَرٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ طَاوُسٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ ⦗ص: 116⦘ عَنْهُمَا، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي الْمَوَاقِيتِ مِثْلَ مَعْنَى حَدِيثِ سُفْيَانَ فِي الْمَوَاقِيتِ

Report491/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 116
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from al-Qasim b. Ma'n, from Layth, from Tawus, from Ibn Abbas, that he said:

Matn

“The Messenger of God ﷺ appointed Dhu al-Hulayfah for the people of Medina, al-Juhfah for the people of Syria, Yalamlam for the people of Yemen, and Qarn for the people of Najd; and whoever is within that [limit], then from where he begins.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مَعْنٍ، عَنْ لَيْثٍ، عَنْ طَاوُسٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

«وَقَّتَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِأَهْلِ الْمَدِينَةِ ذَا الْحُلَيْفَةِ، وَلِأَهْلِ الشَّامِ الْجُحْفَةَ، وَلِأَهْلِ الْيَمَنِ يَلَمْلَمَ، وَلِأَهْلِ نَجْدٍ قَرْنًا، وَمَنْ كَانَ دُونَ ذَلِكَ فَمِنْ حَيْثُ يَبْدَأُ»

Report492/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 116
English
Isnad

Muslim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata', that when the Messenger of God ﷺ appointed the miqats he said:

Matn

“Let a person enjoy his wife and his clothes until he reaches such-and-such” — referring to the miqats.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لَمَّا وَقَّتَ الْمَوَاقِيتَ قَالَ:

Matn

«لِيَسْتَمْتِعِ الْمَرْءُ بِأَهْلِهِ وَثِيَابِهِ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَ كَذَا وَكَذَا». لِلْمَوَاقِيتِ

Report494/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 116
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Ibn Abi Labid, from Muhammad b. Ka'b al-Qurazi or another, who said:

Matn

“Adam ﵇ performed Hajj, and the angels met him and said: ‘Your rite is accepted, Adam; we performed Hajj before you by two thousand years.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي لَبِيدٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ الْقُرَظِيِّ أَوْ غَيْرِهِ، قَالَ:

Matn

" حَجَّ آدَمُ عليه السلام فَلَقِيَتْهُ الْمَلَائِكَةُ فَقَالُوا: بِرَّ نُسُكُكَ آدَمُ، لَقَدْ حَجَجْنَا قَبْلَكَ بِأَلْفَيْ عَامٍ "

English
Isnad

Al-Darawardi and Hatim b. Isma'il informed us, from Ja'far b. Muhammad, from his father, who said:

Matn

We came to Jabir b. Abdullah while he was narrating about the Hajj of the Prophet ﷺ, and he said: When we were at Dhu al-Hulayfah, Asma bint Umays gave birth, and he commanded her to bathe and enter ihram.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا الدَّرَاوَرْدِيُّ، وَحَاتِمُ بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ، عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ قَالَ:

Matn

جِئْنَا جَابِرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ وَهُوَ يُحَدِّثُ عَنْ حَجَّةِ النَّبِيِّ، صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ: فَلَمَّا كُنَّا بِذِي الْحُلَيْفَةِ وَلَدَتْ أَسْمَاءُ بِنْتُ عُمَيْسٍ، فَأَمَرَهَا بِالْغُسْلِ وَالْإِحْرَامِ

Report496/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 116
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Zayd b. Aslam, from Ibrahim b. Abdullah b. Hunayn, from his father, that

Matn

Ibn Abbas and al-Miswar b. Makhramah disagreed at al-Abwa'. Ibn Abbas said: “The muhrim [person in ihram] may wash his head,” and al-Miswar said: “The muhrim may not wash his head.” So Ibn Abbas sent me to Abu Ayyub al-Ansari, and I found him bathing between the two posts [of a well] while screening himself with a cloth. He said: I greeted him, and he said: “Who is this?” I said: “I am Abdullah. Ibn Abbas sent me to you to ask: how did the Messenger of God ﷺ wash his head while in ihram?” He said: Abu Ayyub placed his hands on the cloth and lowered it ⦗p. 117⦘ until his head appeared to me; then he said to a person who was pouring [water] over him: “Pour,” and he poured over his head. Then he moved his head with his hands, bringing them forward and back, and said: “This is how I saw him ﷺ do it.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ حُنَيْنٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ،

Matn

أَنَّ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، وَالْمِسْوَرَ بْنَ مَخْرَمَةَ، اخْتَلَفَا بِالْأَبْوَاءِ، فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ: " يَغْسِلُ الْمُحْرِمُ رَأْسَهُ، وَقَالَ الْمِسْوَرُ: لَا يَغْسِلُ الْمُحْرِمُ رَأْسَهُ، فَأَرْسَلَنِي ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ إِلَى أَبِي أَيُّوبَ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ فَوَجَدْتُهُ يَغْتَسِلُ بَيْنَ الْقَرْنَيْنِ وَهُوَ يَسْتَتِرُ بِثَوْبٍ، قَالَ: فَسَلَّمْتُ فَقَالَ: مَنْ هَذَا؟ فَقُلْتُ: أَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ، أَرْسَلَنِي إِلَيْكَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ أَسْأَلُكُ: كَيْفَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَغْسِلُ رَأْسَهُ وَهُوَ مُحْرِمٌ؟ قَالَ: فَوَضَعَ أَبُو أَيُّوبَ يَدَيْهِ عَلَى الثَّوْبِ فَطَأْطَأَهُ ⦗ص: 117⦘ حَتَّى بَدَا إِلَيَّ رَأْسُهُ، ثُمَّ قَالَ لِإِنْسَانٍ يَصُبُّ عَلَيْهِ: اصْبُبْ، فَصَبَّ عَلَى رَأْسِهِ، ثُمَّ حَرَّكَ رَأْسَهُ بِيَدَيْهِ فَأَقْبَلَ بِهِمَا وَأَدْبَرَ ثُمَّ قَالَ: هَكَذَا رَأَيْتُهُ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَسَلَّمَ يَفْعَلُ "

Report497/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 117
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj: Ata' informed me that Safwan b. Ya'la informed him, from his father, Ya'la b. Umayyah, that he said:

Matn

While Umar b. al-Khattab ﵁ was bathing beside a camel, and I was screening him with a cloth, Umar b. al-Khattab said: “O Ya'la, pour [water] over my head.” I said: “The Commander of the Believers knows best.” Umar b. al-Khattab ﵁ said: “Water does nothing to hair but make it more disheveled.” Then he said the name of God the Exalted and poured it over his head.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي عَطَاءٌ، أَنَّ صَفْوَانَ بْنَ يَعْلَى، أَخْبَرَهُ عَنْ أَبِيهِ، يَعْلَى بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

بَيْنَمَا عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ رضي الله عنه يَغْتَسِلُ إِلَى بَعِيرٍ وَأَنَا أَسْتَرُ عَلَيْهِ بِثَوْبٍ إِذْ قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ: يَا يَعْلَى، أَصْبُبْ عَلَى رَأْسِي، فَقُلْتُ: أَمِيرُ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ أَعْلَمُ، فَقَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ رضي الله عنه: مَا يَزِيدُ الْمَاءُ الشَّعْرَ إِلَّا شَعِثًا، فَسَمَّى اللَّهَ تَعَالَى ثُمَّ أَفَاضَ عَلَى رَأْسِهِ "

English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Abd al-Karim al-Jazari, from Ikrimah, from Ibn Abbas, who said:

Matn

“Umar b. al-Khattab ﵁ would sometimes say to me: ‘Come, let me compete with you in the water, to see which of us can hold his breath longer,’ while we were in ihram.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْكَرِيمِ الْجَزَرِيِّ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ قَالَ:

Matn

" رُبَّمَا قَالَ لِي عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ رضي الله عنه: تَعَالَ أُبَاقِيَكَ فِي الْمَاءِ أَيُّنَا أَطْوَلُ نَفْسًا، وَنَحْنُ مُحْرِمُونَ "

Report499/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 117
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us that he heard Amr b. Dinar say: I heard Abu al-Sha'tha' say: I heard Ibn Abbas say:

Matn

I heard the Messenger of God ﷺ giving a sermon, saying: “If the muhrim cannot find sandals, he may wear leather socks; and if he cannot find a waist-wrapper, he may wear trousers.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ عَمْرَو بْنَ دِينَارٍ، يَقُولُ: سَمِعْتُ أَبَا الشَّعْثَاءِ، يَقُولُ: سَمِعْتُ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، وَهُوَ يَقُولُ:

Matn

سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَخْطُبُ وَهُوَ يَقُولُ: «إِذَا لَمْ يَجِدِ الْمُحْرِمُ نَعْلَيْنِ لَبِسَ الْخُفَّيْنِ، وَإِذَا لَمْ يَجِدْ إِزَارًا لَبِسَ السَّرَاوِيلَ»

Report500/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 117
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Salim, from his father, that

Matn

a man came to the Prophet ﷺ and asked him: What may the muhrim wear of clothing? He said to him: “He may not wear a shirt, nor a turban, nor a hooded cloak, nor trousers, nor leather socks, except for one who cannot find sandals; if he cannot find sandals, let him wear leather socks, and let him cut them down until they are below the ankles.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، أَتَى النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلَهُ: مَا يَلْبَسُ الْمُحْرِمُ مِنَ الثِّيَابِ؟ فَقَالَ لَهُ: «لَا يَلْبَسُ الْقَمِيصَ، وَلَا الْعِمَامَةَ، وَلَا الْبُرْنُسَ، وَلَا السَّرَاوِيلَ، وَلَا الْخُفَّيْنِ، إِلَّا لِمَنْ لَا يَجِدُ النَّعْلَيْنِ، فَإِنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ نَعْلَيْنِ فَلْيَلْبَسْ خُفَّيْنِ، وَلْيَقْطَعْهُمَا حَتَّى يَكُونَا أَسْفَلَ مِنَ الْكَعْبَيْنِ»

Report501/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 117
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Ibn Umar ﵄, that

Matn

a man asked the Prophet ﷺ: What may the muhrim wear of clothing? The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “The muhrim may not wear ⦗p. 118⦘ shirts, nor trousers, nor turbans, nor hooded cloaks, nor leather socks, except someone who cannot find sandals: let him wear leather socks and cut them below the ankles.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ رضي الله عنهما

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، سَأَلَ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم: مَا يَلْبَسُ الْمُحْرِمُ مِنَ الثِّيَابِ؟ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «لَا يَلْبَسُ الْمُحْرِمُ ⦗ص: 118⦘ الْقَمِيصَ، وَلَا السَّرَاوِيلَاتِ، وَلَا الْعَمَائِمَ، وَلَا الْبَرَانِسَ، وَلَا الْخِفَافَ، إِلَّا أَحَدٌ لَا يَجِدُ نَعْلَيْنِ فَلْيَلْبَسِ الْخُفَّيْنِ، وَلْيَقْطَعْهُمَا أَسْفَلَ مِنَ الْكَعْبَيْنِ»

Malik informed us, from Abdullah b. Dinar, from Abdullah b. Umar ﵄, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ forbade the muhrim to wear a garment dyed with saffron or wars [a yellow dye], and said: “Whoever cannot find sandals, let him wear leather socks and cut them below the ankles.”

Isnad

Matn

Report503/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 118
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Amr, from Abu Ja'far, who said:

Matn

“Umar b. al-Khattab ﵁ saw on Abdullah b. Ja'far two garments lightly dyed red while he was in ihram, and said: ‘What are these clothes?’ Ali b. Abi Talib ﵁ said: ‘I do not think anyone is going to teach us the Sunnah.’ So Umar ﵁ fell silent.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ عَمْرٍو، عَنْ أَبِي جَعْفَرٍ قَالَ:

Matn

" أَبْصَرَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ رضي الله عنه عَلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ جَعْفَرٍ ثَوْبَيْنِ مُضَرَّجَيْنِ وَهُوَ مُحْرِمٌ فَقَالَ: " مَا هَذِهِ الثِّيَابُ؟ فَقَالَ عَلِيُّ بْنُ أَبِي طَالِبٍ رضي الله عنه: مَا أَخَالُ أَحَدًا يُعَلِّمُنَا السُّنَّةَ، فَسَكَتَ عُمَرُ رضي الله عنه "

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Abu al-Zubayr, from Jabir ﵁, that he heard him say:

Matn

“A woman may not wear perfumed clothes, but she may wear clothes dyed with safflower, and I do not consider safflower to be perfume.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ رضي الله عنه أَنَّهُ سَمِعَهُ يَقُولُ:

Matn

«لَا تَلْبَسُ الْمَرْأَةُ ثِيَابَ الطِّيبِ، وَتَلْبَسُ الثِّيَابَ الْمُعَصْفَرَةَ، وَلَا أَرَى الْمُعَصْفَرَ طِيبًا»

Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from al-Zuhri, from Salim, from his father, that

Matn

he used to give a fatwa to women, when they entered ihram, to cut down their leather socks, until Safiyyah informed him from A'ishah that she gave a fatwa to women not to cut them, so he stopped [giving that fatwa].

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ يُفْتِي النِّسَاءَ إِذَا أَحْرَمْنَ أَنْ يَقْطَعْنَ الْخُفَّيْنِ، حَتَّى أَخْبَرَتْهُ صَفِيَّةُ عَنْ عَائِشَةَ أَنَّهَا تُفْتِي النِّسَاءَ أَنْ لَا يَقْطَعْنَ، فَانْتَهَى عَنْهُ

Report506/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 118
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ata', from Ibn Abbas, who said:

Matn

“She lets down some of her outer garment [over her face], and does not fasten it.” I said: “And what is ‘does not fasten it’?” He gestured to me as a woman wraps her outer garment, then pointed to the part of the garment on her cheek and said: “She does not cover it by fastening it over her face; that is what does not stay on her. But she lets it hang down over her face as it hangs, and she does not turn it over, nor fasten it, nor fold it back.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ قَالَ:

Matn

" تُدْلِي عَلَيْهَا مِنْ جَلَابِيبِهَا، وَلَا تَضْرِبُ بِهِ. قُلْتُ: وَمَا لَا تَضْرِبُ بِهِ، فَأَشَارَ لِي كَمَا تُجَلْبِبُ الْمَرْأَةُ، ثُمَّ أَشَارَ إِلَى مَا عَلَى خَدِّهَا مِنَ الْجِلْبَابِ فَقَالَ: لَا تُغَطِّيهِ فَتَضْرِبُ بِهِ عَلَى وَجْهِهَا، فَذَلِكَ الَّذِي لَا يَبْقَى عَلَيْهَا، وَلَكِنْ تَسْدُلُهُ عَلَى وَجْهِهَا كَمَا هُوَ مَسْدُولًا، وَلَا تَقْلِبُهُ، وَلَا تَضْرِبُ بِهِ، وَلَا تُعْطِفُهُ "

  2. 02Ata'عَطَاءٍ

Report511/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 119
English
Isnad

Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said: Al-Hasan b. Muslim informed us, from Safiyyah bint Shaybah, that she said:

Matn

“I was with A'ishah when a woman from the women of Banu Abd al-Dar, called Tamlik, came to her and said: ‘O Mother of the Believers, my daughter so-and-so has sworn not to wear her jewelry during the Hajj season.’ A'ishah ﵂ said: ‘Tell her: The Mother of the Believers adjures you to wear all of your jewelry.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنَا الْحَسَنُ بْنُ مُسْلِمٍ، عَنْ صَفِيَّةَ بِنْتِ شَيْبَةَ، أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ:

Matn

" كُنْتُ عِنْدَ عَائِشَةَ إِذْ جَاءَتْهَا امْرَأَةٌ مِنْ نِسَاءِ بَنِي عَبْدِ الدَّارِ يُقَالُ لَهَا: تَمْلِكُ، قَالَتْ لَهَا: يَا أُمَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، إِنَّ ابْنَتِي فُلَانَةَ حَلَفَتْ أَنْ لَا تَلْبَسَ حُلِيَّهَا فِي الْمَوْسِمِ، فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ رضي الله عنها: قُولِي لَهَا: إِنَّ أُمَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ تُقْسِمُ عَلَيْكِ إِلَّا لَبِسْتِ حُلِيَّكِ كُلَّهُ "

Report512/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 119
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Ayyub b. Musa, from Nafi', from Ibn Umar ﵄, that

Matn

when he had sore eyes while in ihram he would drip aloe (sabr) into his eyes, and that he said: “He may apply kohl, with any kohl, if he has sore eyes, as long as he does not apply a perfumed kohl or [apply it] without an eye ailment.” Ibn Umar was the one who said it.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ بْنِ مُوسَى، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ رضي الله عنهما

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ إِذَا رَمِدَ وَهُوَ مُحْرِمٌ أَقْطَرَ فِي عَيْنَيْهِ الصَّبْرَ إِقْطَارًا، وَأَنَّهُ قَالَ: «يَكْتَحِلُ بِأَيِّ كُحْلٍ إِذَا رَمِدَ مَا لَمْ يَكْتَحِلْ بِطِيبٍ وَمِنْ غَيْرِ رَمَدٍ» ابْنُ عُمَرَ الْقَائِلُ

Report513/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 119
English
Isnad

Ibn Uyaynah informed us, from Amr b. Dinar, from Salim b. Abdullah, who said:

Matn

A'ishah ⦗p. 120⦘ ﵂ said: “I perfumed the Messenger of God ﷺ,” and he [al-Shafi'i] said in the Book of al-Imla': “for his release and for his ihram.” Salim said: “And the Sunnah of the Messenger of God ﷺ is more deserving to be followed.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ قَالَ:

Matn

قَالَتْ ⦗ص: 120⦘ عَائِشَةُ رضي الله عنها: «أَنَا طَيَّبْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم» وَقَالَ فِي كِتَابِ الْإِمْلَاءِ: لِحِلِّهِ وَإِحْرَامِهِ. قَالَ سَالِمٌ: وَسُنَّةُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَحَقُّ أَنْ تُتَّبَعَ

«كُنْتُ أُطَيِّبُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ لِإِحْرَامِهِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُحْرِمَ، وَلِحِلِّهِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَطُوفَ بِالْبَيْتِ»

Report516/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 120
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Abd al-Rahman b. al-Qasim, from al-Qasim b. Muhammad, who said: I heard A'ishah ﵂, spreading out her hands, saying:

Matn

“I perfumed the Messenger of God ﷺ with these two hands of mine for his ihram when he entered ihram, and for his release before he circumambulated the House.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها وَقَدْ بَسَطَتْ يَدَيْهَا تَقُولُ:

Matn

«أَنَا طَيَّبْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِيَدَيَّ هَاتَيْنِ لِإِحْرَامِهِ حِينَ أَحْرَمَ وَلِحِلِّهِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَطُوفَ بِالْبَيْتِ»

Matn

Report518/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 120
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. Uyaynah informed us, from Uthman b. Urwah, who said: I heard my father say: I heard A'ishah ﵂ say:

Matn

“I perfumed the Messenger of God ﷺ for his sacred state and for his release.” I said to her: “With what perfume?” She said: “With the finest perfume.” Uthman said: “Hisham did not narrate this hadith except from me.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ قَالَ: سَمِعْتُ أَبِي يَقُولُ: سَمِعْتُ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها تَقُولُ:

Matn

" طَيَّبْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم لِحُرْمِهِ وَلِحِلِّهِ، فَقُلْتُ لَهَا: بِأَيِّ الطِّيبِ؟ فَقَالَتْ: بِأَطْيَبِ الطِّيبِ " قَالَ عُثْمَانُ: مَا رَوَى هِشَامٌ هَذَا الْحَدِيثَ إِلَّا عَنِّي

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01A'ishahEarliest transmitterعَائِشَةَ
  2. 02my fatherأَبِي
  3. 03Uthman b. Urwahعُثْمَانَ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ
  4. 04Sufyan b. Uyaynahسُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ

English
Isnad

Sufyan b. Uyaynah informed us, from Ata' b. al-Sa'ib, from Ibrahim, from al-Aswad, from A'ishah ﵂, who said:

Matn

“I saw the gleam of perfume in the parting of the hair of the Messenger of God ﷺ after three [days].”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ السَّائِبِ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، عَنِ الْأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ رضي الله عنها قَالَتْ:

Matn

«رَأَيْتُ وَبِيصَ الطِّيبِ فِي مَفَارِقِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَعْدَ ثَلَاثٍ»

Matn

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Hasan b. Zayd, from his father, who said:

Matn

Report524/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 121
English
Isnad

Sufyan informed us, from Amr b. Dinar, from Ata' b. Abi Rabah, from Safwan b. Ya'la b. Umayyah, from his father, who said:

Matn

We were with the Messenger of God ﷺ at al-Ji'ranah when a man came to him wearing a cut garment (muqatta'ah) — meaning a jubbah [cloak] — and smeared with khaluq [a saffron-based perfume]. He said: “O Messenger of God, I have entered ihram for 'Umrah and this is on me.” The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “What would you do in your Hajj?” He said: “I would take off this garment and wash off this khaluq.” The Messenger of God ﷺ said: “Then whatever you would do in your Hajj, do it in your 'Umrah.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ أَبِي رَبَاحٍ، عَنْ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ يَعْلَى بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ قَالَ:

Matn

كُنَّا عِنْدَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِالْجِعْرَانَةِ، فَأَتَاهُ رَجُلٌ وَعَلَيْهِ مُقَطَّعَةٌ، يَعْنِي جُبَّةً، وَهُوَ مُتَضَمِّخٌ بِالْخَلُوقِ، فَقَالَ: يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ، إِنِّي أَحْرَمْتُ بِالْعُمْرَةِ وَهَذِهِ عَلَيَّ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «مَا كُنْتَ صَانِعًا فِي حَجِّكِ؟» قَالَ: كُنْتُ أَنْزِعُ هَذِهِ الْمُقَطَّعَةَ وَأَغْسِلُ هَذَا الْخَلُوقَ، فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «فَمَا كُنْتَ صَانِعًا فِي حَجَّتِكَ فَاصْنَعْهُ فِي عُمْرَتِكَ»

Transmission overview

1 path

Transmission path

Earliest to latest
  1. 01his fatherEarliest transmitterأَبِيهِ
  2. 02Safwan b. Ya'la b. Umayyahصَفْوَانَ بْنِ يَعْلَى بْنِ أُمَيَّةَ
  3. 03Ata' b. Abi Rabahعَطَاءِ بْنِ أَبِي رَبَاحٍ
  4. 04Amr b. Dinarعَمْرِو بْنِ دِينَارٍ
  5. 05Sufyanسُفْيَانُ

English
Isnad

Isma'il, who is known as Ibn Ulayyah, informed us: Abd al-Aziz b. Suhayb informed me, from Anas b. Malik ﵁, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ forbade a man to perfume himself with saffron.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِسْمَاعِيلُ الَّذِي يُعْرَفُ بِابْنِ عُلَيَّةَ، أَخْبَرَنِي عَبْدُ الْعَزِيزِ بْنُ صُهَيْبٍ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ رضي الله عنه،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى أَنْ يَتَزَعْفَرَ الرَّجُلُ

Report527/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 121
English
Isnad

Muslim b. Khalid informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said:

Matn

I said to Nafi': “Did you hear Abdullah b. ⦗p. 122⦘ Umar name the months of Hajj?” He said: “Yes, he used to name them: Shawwal, Dhu al-Qa'dah and Dhu al-Hijjah.” He said: I said to Nafi': “And if someone enters ihram for Hajj before them?” He said: “I did not hear anything from him about that.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ:

Matn

قُلْتُ نَافِعُ أَسَمِعْتَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ ⦗ص: 122⦘ عُمَرَ يُسَمِّي أَشْهُرَ الْحَجِّ؟ فَقَالَ: " نَعَمْ، كَانَ يُسَمِّي: شَوَّالٌ وَذُو الْقَعْدَةِ وَذُو الْحِجَّةِ. قَالَ: قُلْتُ لِنَافِعٍ: فَإِنْ أَهَلَّ إِنْسَانٌ بِالْحَجِّ قَبْلَهُنَّ؟ قَالَ: لَمْ أَسْمَعْ مِنْهُ فِي ذَلِكَ شَيْئًا "

  2. 02Muslim b. Khalidمُسْلِمُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ

English
Isnad

Ibrahim b. Muhammad informed us, from Sa'id ibn Abd al-Rahman b. Ruqaysh, that Jabir b. Abdullah ﵄ said:

Matn

“The Messenger of God ﷺ never named Hajj or 'Umrah in his talbiyah.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ رُقَيْشٍ، أَنَّ جَابِرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ رضي الله عنهما قَالَ:

Matn

«مَا سَمَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي تَلْبِيَتِهِ حَجًّا قَطُّ وَلَا عُمْرَةً»

Report529/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 122
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Ibn Umar ﵄, that

Matn

the talbiyah of the Messenger of God ﷺ was: “Labbayk [Here I am at Your service], O God, labbayk; labbayk, You have no partner, labbayk. Indeed, praise and blessing are Yours, and dominion; You have no partner.” Nafi' said: And Abdullah b. Umar used to add to it: “Labbayk, labbayk, and sa'dayk [at Your pleasure]; all good is in Your hands; desire is toward You, and [all] action.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ رضي الله عنهما

Matn

أَنْ تَلْبِيَةَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «لَبَّيْكَ اللَّهُمَّ لَبَّيْكَ، لَبَّيْكَ لَا شَرِيكَ لَكَ لَبَّيْكَ، إِنَّ الْحَمْدَ وَالنِّعْمَةَ لَكَ وَالْمُلْكَ، لَا شَرِيكَ لَكَ» قَالَ نَافِعٌ: وَكَانَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ يَزِيدُ فِيهَا: لَبَّيْكَ لَبَّيْكَ وَسَعْدَيْكَ، وَالْخَيْرُ فِي يَدَيْكَ، وَالرَّغْبَاءُ إِلَيْكَ وَالْعَمَلُ

Report530/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 122
English
Isnad

Some of the people of knowledge informed us, from Ja'far b. Muhammad, from his father, from Jabir b. Abdullah ﵄, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ entered ihram with the declaration of oneness: “Labbayk, O God, labbayk; You have no partner, labbayk. Indeed, praise and blessing are Yours, and dominion; You have no partner.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا بَعْضُ، أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ رضي الله عنهما

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَهَلَّ بِالتَّوْحِيدِ: «لَبَّيْكَ اللَّهُمَّ لَبَّيْكَ، لَا شَرِيكَ لَكَ لَبَّيْكَ، إِنَّ الْحَمْدَ وَالنِّعْمَةَ لَكَ وَالْمُلْكَ، لَا شَرِيكَ لَكَ»

Report531/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 122
English
Isnad

Al-Shafi'i ﵁ said: Abd al-Aziz b. Abdullah al-Majishun mentioned, from Abdullah b. al-Fadl, from al-A'raj, from Abu Hurayrah ﵁, who said:

Matn

Among the talbiyah of the Messenger of God ﷺ was: “Labbayk, God of Truth, labbayk.”

العربية
Isnad

قَالَ الشَّافِعِيُّ رضي الله عنه: وَذَكَرَ عَبْدُ الْعَزِيزِ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الْمَاجِشُونُ عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْفَضْلِ، عَنِ الْأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنه قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ مِنْ تَلْبِيَةِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم: «لَبَّيْكَ إِلَهَ الْحَقِّ لَبَّيْكَ»

Report532/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 122
English
Isnad

Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said: Humayd al-A'raj informed me, from Mujahid, that he said:

Matn

The Prophet ﷺ used to say aloud in the talbiyah: “Labbayk, O God, labbayk; labbayk, You have no partner, labbayk. Indeed, praise and blessing are Yours, and dominion; You have no partner.” He said: Until one day, as the people were dispersing from around him, as though what he was in pleased him, he added to it: “Labbayk — truly, the [true] life is the life of the Hereafter.” Ibn Jurayj said: And I think that was on the day of Arafah.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي حُمَيْدُ الْأَعْرَجُ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

كَانَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُظْهِرُ مِنَ التَّلْبِيَةِ: «لَبَّيْكَ اللَّهُمَّ لَبَّيْكَ، لَبَّيْكَ لَا شَرِيكَ لَكَ لَبَّيْكَ، إِنَّ الْحَمْدَ وَالنِّعْمَةَ لَكَ وَالْمُلْكَ، لَا شَرِيكَ لَكَ». قَالَ: حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَ ذَاتَ يَوْمٍ وَالنَّاسُ يُصْرَفُونَ عَنْهُ كَأَنَّهُ أَعْجَبَهُ مَا هُوَ فِيهِ فَزَادَ فِيهَا: «لَبَّيْكَ إِنَّ الْعَيْشَ عَيْشُ الْآخِرَةِ» قَالَ ابْنُ جُرَيْجٍ: وَحَسِبْتُ أَنَّ ذَلِكَ يَوْمَ عَرَفَةَ

Transmission path

  2. 02Humayd al-A'rajحُمَيْدُ الْأَعْرَجُ

Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 123
English
Isnad

The translation is shown whole: its chain opens the passage below.

Matn

Sa'id informed us, from al-Qasim b. Ma'n, from Muhammad b. Ajlan, from Abdullah b. Abi Salamah, that he said: Sa'd b. Abi Waqqas heard one of his nephews saying in the talbiyah: “O Lord of the Ascents!” Sa'd said: “The Ascents — He is indeed the Lord of the Ascents; but this is not how we used to say the talbiyah in the time of the Messenger of God ﷺ.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدٌ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مَعْنٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَجْلَانَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ: سَمِعَ سَعْدُ بْنُ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ،

Matn

بَعْضَ بَنِي أَخِيهِ وَهُوَ يُلَبِّي: يَا ذَا الْمَعَارِجِ، فَقَالَ سَعْدٌ: «الْمَعَارِجُ إِنَّهُ لَذُو الْمَعَارِجِ، وَمَا هَكَذَا كُنَّا نُلَبِّي عَلَى عَهْدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم»

Report534/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 123
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Abdullah b. Abi Bakr b. Muhammad b. Amr b. Hazm, from Abd al-Malik b. Abi Bakr b. Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith b. Hisham, from Khallad b. al-Sa'ib al-Ansari, from his father, that the Messenger of God ﷺ said:

Matn

“Gabriel ﵇ came to me and commanded me to command my companions, or those with me, to raise their voices in the talbiyah, or in the ihlal.” He means one of the two.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ خَلَّادِ بْنِ السَّائِبِ الْأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ:

Matn

«أَتَانِي جِبْرِيلُ عليه السلام فَأَمَرَنِي أَنْ آمُرَ أَصْحَابِي، أَوْ مَنْ مَعِيَ أَنْ يَرْفَعُوا أَصْوَاتَهُمْ بِالتَّلْبِيَةِ، أَوْ بِالْإِهْلَالِ». يُرِيدُ أَحَدَهُمَا

  3. 03Abd al-Malik b. Abi Bakr b. Abd al-Rahman b. al-Harith b. Hishamعَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ

Report537/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 123
English
Isnad

Ibrahim b. Muhammad informed us, from Salih b. Muhammad b. Za'idah, from Umarah b. Khuzaymah b. Thabit, from his father, from the Prophet ﷺ, that

Matn

when he finished his talbiyah he would ask God for His good pleasure and Paradise, and seek, by His mercy, deliverance from the Fire.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ صَالِحِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ زَائِدَةَ، عَنْ عُمَارَةَ بْنِ خُزَيْمَةَ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم

Matn

أَنَّهُ كَانَ إِذَا فَرَغَ مِنْ تَلْبِيَتِهِ سَأَلَ اللَّهَ رِضْوَانَهُ وَالْجَنَّةَ وَاسْتَعْفَاهُ بِرَحْمَتِهِ مِنَ النَّارِ

Report538/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 123
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. Uyaynah informed us, from Hisham b. Urwah, from his father, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ passed by Duba'ah bint al-Zubayr and said: “Do you not want to perform Hajj?” She said: “I am ill.” He said to her: “Perform Hajj and stipulate that ‘my place of release is where You confine me.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مَرَّ بِضُبَاعَةَ بِنْتِ الزُّبَيْرِ فَقَالَ: «أَمَا تُرِيدِينَ الْحَجَّ؟» فَقَالَتْ: إِنِّي شَاكِيَةٌ، فَقَالَ لَهَا: «حُجِّي وَاشْتَرِطِي أَنَّ مَحِلِّي حَيْثُ حَبَسْتَنِي»

Report539/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 123
English
Isnad

Sufyan b. Uyaynah informed us, from Hisham, from his father, who said:

Matn

“A'ishah said to me: ‘Do you make an exception ⦗p. 124⦘ when you perform Hajj?’ I said to her: ‘What do I say?’ She said: ‘Say: O God, I intended Hajj and resolved upon it; if You make it easy, then it is the Hajj, and if something detains me, then it is an 'Umrah.’”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ قَالَ:

Matn

" قَالَتْ لِي عَائِشَةُ: هَلْ تَسْتَثْنِي ⦗ص: 124⦘ إِذَا حَجَجْتَ؟ فَقُلْتُ لَهَا: مَاذَا أَقُولُ؟ فَقَالَتْ: قُلِ: اللَّهُمَّ الْحَجَّ أَرَدْتُ، وَلَهُ عَمَدْتُ، فَإِنْ يَسَّرْتَهُ فَهُوَ الْحَجُّ، وَإِنْ حَبَسَنِي حَابِسٌ فَهِيَ عُمْرَةٌ "

Report540/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 124
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Nafi', from Ibn Umar, that

Matn

he went out to Mecca in the time of the fitnah [civil strife] intending 'Umrah and said: “If I am prevented from the House, we will do as we did with the Messenger of God ﷺ.” Al-Shafi'i ﵁ said: He means: we would come out of ihram as we came out of ihram with the Messenger of God ﷺ in the year of al-Hudaybiyah.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ،

Matn

أَنَّهُ خَرَجَ إِلَى مَكَّةَ زَمَنَ الْفِتْنَةِ مُعْتَمِرًا فَقَالَ: «إِنْ صُدِدْتُ عَنِ الْبَيْتِ صَنَعْنَا كَمَا صَنَعْنَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم» قَالَ الشَّافِعِيُّ رضي الله عنه: يَعْنِي أَحْلَلْنَا كَمَا أَحْلَلْنَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَامَ الْحُدَيْبِيَةِ

Isnad

Malik informed us, from Ibn Shihab, from Salim b. Abdullah, from his father, who said:

Matn

“Whoever is held back from the House by illness does not come out of ihram until he circumambulates the House and between al-Safa and al-Marwah.”

العربية
Isnad

Matn

Report543/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 124
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Yahya b. Sa'id, from Sulayman b. Yasar, that

Matn

Ibn Umar, Marwan and Ibn al-Zubayr gave a fatwa to Ibn Huzabah al-Makhzumi, who had collapsed on some part of the road to Mecca while in ihram, that he should treat himself with what was unavoidable and pay a ransom; and when he recovered, perform 'Umrah and thereby come out of his ihram, and he must perform Hajj the following year and offer a sacrificial animal.

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ ابْنَ عُمَرَ، وَمَرْوَانَ، وَابْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَفْتَوُا ابْنَ حُزَابَةَ الْمَخْزُومِيَّ وَإِنَّهُ صُرِعَ بِبَعْضِ طَرِيقِ مَكَّةَ وَهُوَ مُحْرِمٌ أَنْ يَتَدَاوَى بِمَا لَا بُدَّ مِنْهُ وَيَفْتَدِيَ، فَإِذَا صَحَّ اعْتَمَرَ فَحَلَّ مِنْ إِحْرَامِهِ، وَكَانَ عَلَيْهِ أَنْ يَحُجَّ عَامًا قَابِلًا وَيُهْدِي

Report544/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 124
English
Isnad

Anas b. Iyad informed us, from Musa b. Uqbah, from Nafi', from Ibn Umar, that he said:

Matn

“Whoever among the pilgrims reaches the night of the Sacrifice and stands opposite Arafah before dawn breaks has caught the Hajj; and whoever does not reach Arafah and stand there before dawn breaks has missed the Hajj. Let him come to the House and circumambulate it seven times, and perform the circuit between al-Safa and al-Marwah seven times, then let him shave or trim if he wishes; and if he has his sacrificial animal with him, let him slaughter it before he shaves. When he finishes his circumambulation and his sa'y, let him shave or trim, then return to his family. If the Hajj of the next year reaches him, let him perform Hajj if he is able, and let him offer a camel ⦗p. 125⦘ as a sacrifice; and if he cannot find a sacrificial animal, let him fast three days during the Hajj and seven when he returns to his family.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا أَنَسُ بْنُ عِيَاضٍ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ:

Matn

" مَنْ أَدْرَكَ لَيْلَةَ النَّحْرِ مِنَ الْحَاجِّ فَوَقَفَ بِحِيَالِ عَرَفَةَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَطْلُعَ الْفَجْرُ فَقَدْ أَدْرَكَ الْحَجَّ، وَمَنْ لَمْ يُدْرِكْ عَرَفَةَ فَوَقَفَ بِهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَطْلُعَ الْفَجْرُ فَقَدْ فَاتَهُ الْحَجُّ، فَلْيَأْتِ الْبَيْتَ فَلْيَطُفْ بِهِ سَبْعًا، وَيَطُوفُ بَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ سَبْعًا، ثُمَّ لِيَحْلِقْ أَوْ يَقْصُرْ إِنْ شَاءَ، وَإِنْ كَانَ مَعَهُ هَدْيُهُ فَلْيَنْحَرْهُ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَحْلِقَ، فَإِذَا فَرَغَ مِنْ طَوَافِهِ وَسَعْيِهِ فَلْيَحْلِقْ أَوْ يَقْصُرْ، ثُمَّ لِيَرْجِعْ إِلَى أَهْلِهِ، فَإِنْ أَدْرَكَهُ الْحَجُّ قَابِلَ فَلْيَحْجُجْ إِنِ اسْتَطَاعَ، وَلْيُهْدِ بَدَنَةً ⦗ص: 125⦘ ، فَإِنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ هَدْيًا فَلْيَصُمْ عَنْهُ ثَلَاثَةَ أَيَّامٍ فِي الْحَجِّ وَسَبْعَةً إِذَا رَجَعَ إِلَى أَهْلِهِ

Report545/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 125
English
Isnad

Malik informed us, from Yahya b. Sa'id, who said: Sulayman b. Yasar informed me that

Matn

Abu Ayyub set out on Hajj, and when he was in the desert on the road to Mecca he lost his mounts, and he came to Umar b. al-Khattab on the Day of Sacrifice and mentioned that to him. [Umar] said to him: “Do as the person performing 'Umrah does, and you will have come out of ihram; and when the next Hajj reaches you, perform Hajj and offer what is easily available of sacrificial animals.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ قَالَ: أَخْبَرَنِي سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ يَسَارٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ أَبَا أَيُّوبَ، خَرَجَ حَاجًّا حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَ بِالْبَادِيَةِ مِنْ طَرِيقِ مَكَّةَ أَضَلَّ رَوَاحِلَهُ، وَأَنَّهُ قَدِمَ عَلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ يَوْمَ لنَّحْرِ فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَقَالَ لَهُ: «اصْنَعْ كَمَا يَصْنَعُ الْمُعْتَمِرُ ثُمَّ قَدْ حَلَلْتَ، فَإِذَا أَدْرَكْتَ الْحَجَّ قَابِلَ فَحُجَّ وَأَهْدِ مَا اسْتَيْسَرَ مِنَ الْهَدْيِ»

Report548/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 125
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, that

Matn

the Messenger of God ﷺ, when he saw the House, would raise his hands and say: “O God, increase this House in honor, in glorification, in esteem and in awe; and increase in honor and nobility, among those who perform Hajj or 'Umrah to it, in honor, esteem, glorification and piety.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ إِذَا رَأَى الْبَيْتَ رَفَعَ يَدَيْهِ وَقَالَ: «اللَّهُمَّ زِدْ هَذَا الْبَيْتَ تَشْرِيفًا، وَتَعْظِيمًا، وَتَكْرِيمًا، وَمَهَابَةً، وَزِدْ مِنْ شَرَفِهِ، وَكَرَمِهِ مِمَّنْ حَجَّهُ وَاعْتَمَرَهُ تَشْرِيفًا وَتَكْرِيمًا وَتَعْظِيمًا وَبِرًّا»

  2. 02Sa'id ibn Salimسَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ

English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said:

Matn

I was told, from Miqsam, freedman of Abdullah b. al-Harith, from Ibn Abbas ﵄, from the Prophet ﷺ, that he said: “Hands are raised in prayer, and when the House is seen, and on al-Safa and al-Marwah, and on the evening of Arafah, and at Jam' [Muzdalifah], and at the two Jamrahs, and over the dead.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ:

Matn

حُدِّثْتُ عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ، مَوْلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رضي الله عنهما، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ قَالَ: «تُرْفَعُ الْأَيْدِي فِي الصَّلَاةِ، وَإِذَا رُئِيَ الْبَيْتُ، وَعَلَى الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ، وَعَشِيَّةَ عَرَفَةَ، وَبِجَمْعٍ، وَعِنْدَ الْجَمْرَتَيْنِ، وَعَلَى الْمَيِّتِ»

Isnad

Sufyan b. Uyaynah informed us, from Mansur, from Abu Wa'il, from Masruq, from Abdullah b. Mas'ud, that

Matn

he saw him begin by touching the Stone, then take to his right and jog (ramal) three circuits and walk four; then he came to the Maqam and prayed two rak'ahs behind it.

العربية
Isnad

Matn

أَنَّهُ رَآهُ بَدَأَ فَاسْتَلَمَ الْحَجَرَ، ثُمَّ أَخَذَ عَنْ يَمِينِهِ فَرَمَلَ ثَلَاثَةَ أَطْوَافٍ وَمَشَى أَرْبَعَةً، ثُمَّ أَنَّهُ أَتَى الْمَقَامَ فَصَلَّى خَلْفَهُ رَكْعَتَيْنِ

Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, from Abu Ja'far, who said:

Matn

“I saw Ibn Abbas come on the Day of Tarwiyah [the 8th of Dhu al-Hijjah] with his hair matted, and he kissed the Corner, then prostrated upon it, then kissed it and prostrated upon it, three times.”

العربية
Isnad

«رَأَيْتُ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ جَاءَ يَوْمَ التَّرْوِيَةِ مُسَبِّدًا رَأْسَهُ، فَقَبَّلَ الرُّكْنَ ثُمَّ سَجَدَ عَلَيْهِ، ثُمَّ قَبَّلَهُ ثُمَّ سَجَدَ عَلَيْهِ، ثَلَاثَ مَرَّاتٍ»

Report555/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 126
English
Isnad

Sa'id informed us, from Ibn Jurayj, who said:

Matn

I said to Ata': “Did you see any of the companions of the Messenger of God ﷺ kiss their hands when they touched [the Stone]?” He said: “Yes. I saw Jabir b. Abdullah, Ibn Umar, Abu Sa'id al-Khudri and Abu Hurayrah ﵃, when they touched it, kiss their hands.” I said: “And Ibn Abbas?” He said: “Yes, and I think many.” I said: “Do you leave off kissing your hand when you touch it?” He said: “Then why would I touch it at all?”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ جُرَيْجٍ قَالَ:

Matn

قُلْتُ لِعَطَاءٍ: هَلْ رَأَيْتَ أَحَدًا مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِذَا اسْتَلَمُوا قَبَّلُوا أَيْدِيَهُمْ؟ فَقَالَ: " نَعَمْ، رَأَيْتُ جَابِرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، وَابْنَ عُمَرَ، وَأَبَا سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيَّ، وَأَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ رضي الله عنهم، إِذَا اسْتَلَمُوا قَبَّلُوا أَيْدِيَهُمْ. قُلْتُ: وَابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ؟ قَالَ: نَعَمْ، وَحَسِبْتُ كَثِيرًا، قُلْتُ: هَلْ تَدَعُ أَنْتَ إِذَا اسْتَلَمْتَ أَنْ تُقَبِّلَ يَدَكَ؟ قَالَ: فَلِمَ أَسْتَلِمُهُ إِذًا؟ "

Report556/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 126
English
Isnad

Sa'id informed us, from Musa b. Ubaydah, from Muhammad b. Ka'b, that

Matn

a man from among the companions of the Messenger of God ﷺ used to touch all the corners and say: “It is not fitting that any part of the House of God the Exalted be abandoned.” And Ibn Abbas used to say: “You have in the Messenger of God an excellent example.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدٌ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُبَيْدَةَ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ رَجُلًا، مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يَمْسَحُ الْأَرْكَانَ كُلَّهَا وَيَقُولُ: لَا يَنْبَغِي لِبَيْتِ اللَّهِ تَعَالَى أَنْ يَكُونَ شَيْءٌ مِنْهُ مَهْجُورًا. وَكَانَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ يَقُولُ: «لَقَدْ كَانَ لَكُمْ فِي رَسُولِ اللَّهِ أُسْوَةٌ حَسَنَةٌ»

Report558/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 127
English
Isnad

Sa'id ibn Salim informed us, from Umar b. Sa'id ibn Abi Husayn, from Manbudh b. Abi Sulayman, from his mother, that

Matn

she was with A'ishah, wife of the Prophet ﷺ, Mother of the Believers, when a freedwoman of hers came in to her and said: “O Mother of the Believers, I circumambulated the House seven times and touched the Corner twice or three times.” A'ishah said to her: “May God not reward you, may God not reward you! You push against the men! Why did you not say takbir and pass by?”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدُ بْنُ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ سَعِيدِ بْنِ أَبِي حُسَيْنٍ، عَنْ مَنْبُوذِ بْنِ أَبِي سُلَيْمَانَ، عَنْ أُمِّهِ،

Matn

أَنَّهَا كَانَتْ عِنْدَ عَائِشَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، فَدَخَلَتْ عَلَيْهَا مَوْلَاةٌ لَهَا فَقَالَتْ لَهَا: " يَا أُمَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، طُفْتُ بِالْبَيْتِ سَبْعًا، وَاسْتَلَمْتُ الرُّكْنَ مَرَّتَيْنِ أَوْ ثَلَاثًا، فَقَالَتْ لَهَا عَائِشَةُ: لَا آجَرَكِ اللَّهُ، لَا آجَرَكِ اللَّهُ، تُدَافِعِينَ الرِّجَالَ أَلَا كَبَّرْتِ وَمَرَرْتِ "

Report559/ 159
Musnad al-Shāfiʿī · Vol. 1, p. 127
English
Isnad

Sa'id informed us: Musa b. Ubaydah al-Rabadhi informed me, from Muhammad b. Ka'b, that

Matn

Ibn Abbas used to touch the Yemeni Corner and the Stone, while Ibn al-Zubayr used to touch all the corners and say: “It is not fitting that any part of the House of God be abandoned.” And Ibn Abbas used to say: “You have in the Messenger of God an excellent example.”

العربية
Isnad

أَخْبَرَنَا سَعِيدٌ، أَخْبَرَنِي مُوسَى بْنُ عُبَيْدَةَ الرَّبَذِيُّ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ،

Matn

أَنَّ ابْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، كَانَ يَمْسَحُ عَلَى الرُّكْنِ الْيَمَانِيِّ وَالْحَجَرِ، وَكَانَ ابْنُ الزُّبَيْرِ يَمْسَحُ الْأَرْكَانَ كُلَّهَا وَيَقُولُ: «لَا يَنْبَغِي لِبَيْتِ اللَّهِ أَنْ يَكُونَ شَيْءٌ مِنْهُ مَهْجُورًا». وَكَانَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ يَقُولُ: «لَقَدْ كَانَ لَكُمْ فِي رَسُولِ اللَّهِ أُسْوَةٌ حَسَنَةٌ»

  2. 02Musa b. Ubaydah al-Rabadhiمُوسَى بْنُ عُبَيْدَةَ الرَّبَذِيُّ
  3. 03Sa'idسَعِيدٌ
